# Skills · O · part 27

870 entries, outline-coach to overbuilder-review. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **outline-coach**: Act as an assistive outline coach who guides structural development through questions. Use when helping someone develop their own outline through diagnosis and frameworks. Critical constraint - never generate outline content. Instead ask questions, identify structural issues, suggest approaches, and let the writer structure. ([jwynia/agent-skills](https://github.com/jwynia/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/creative/fiction/structure/outline-coach) · ★159 · 474 installs)

([jwynia/agent-skills](https://github.com/jwynia/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/creative/fiction/structure/outline-collaborator) · ★159 · 442 installs)
- **outline-concept**: 故事构思工具，快速完成一句话概括（步骤1）→五句式大纲（步骤2）的构思期。当用户说"构思故事"、"验证创意"、"故事概念"、"帮我想一个故事"、"outline"时自动激活。适合快速验证创意，不走完整雪花流程。可独立使用，也可在 snowflake-fiction 主流程中被调用。 ([hestudy/snowflake-fiction](https://github.com/hestudy/snowflake-fiction/tree/HEAD/skills/outline-concept) · ★33)
- **outline-construction**: Use when designing a paper's argumentative skeleton from a findings inventory and a chosen thesis, before any prose is written. ([Nugkta/paper-writer](https://github.com/Nugkta/paper-writer/tree/HEAD/skills/outline-construction))
- **outline-deploy-do**: Deploy an Outline VPN server to DigitalOcean. Use when the user asks to deploy, set up, install, or provision an Outline VPN or Outline proxy server on DigitalOcean. Guides through a structured interview (API token, region, droplet size, name, SSH key, project, state directory), shows a confirmation summary, then executes the full deployment via scripts/deploy.sh. Creates a local state directory with spec.json, state.json, a deployment.md log, and management.json with the Outline Manager API URL and certificate. ([OutlineFoundation/outline-skills](https://github.com/OutlineFoundation/outline-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/outline-deploy-do) · ★1)
- **outline-drafter**: Turn the gap analysis into an outline that fills the gap, answers real questions, and is built to be cited. Sets the sections from table-stakes plus the unique angle, turns PAA into question-form H2s, places the 40-60 word answer capsule and the cited stats up top, plans the one unique E-E-A-T story, and forces an opening that doesn't match the site's other articles. Run after competitor-analyzer, before draft-writer. Produces an outline and stops. ([robdasi/skills](https://github.com/robdasi/skills/tree/HEAD/content-pipeline/outline-drafter))
- **outline-driven-dev**: Enforce Outline-Driven Development (ODD): requirement analysis -&gt; task outline -&gt; incremental execution -&gt; empirical verification. - **outline-expand**: Map-reduce engine — given an outline file and a `paper.json`, draft N sections in parallel by dispatching `@paper-writer` once per section in a single Agent-tool batch, then assemble the prose into each section's `paper.json.sections[name].path`. Passes a shared `nomenclature.md` payload to every dispatch (Phase 2 decision §4) so parallel writers share terminology. After merge, emits a non-blocking `terminology-drift.md` lint artifact listing terms that disagree across sections. First drafts only — does **not** call `/iterate-revision`. Safe to re-run; safe to scope with `--sections`. ([youngeun1209/oh-my-claudecode-research](https://github.com/youngeun1209/oh-my-claudecode-research/tree/HEAD/skills/outline-expand) · ★31)
